Audit script (temporary, archived here for reproducibility; final version disables local WebSocket proxy and uses declared pointcloud__neon_.enable):
import base64,json,time,threading
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np,cv2,rclpy
from rclpy.qos import qos_profile_sensor_data,QoSProfile,DurabilityPolicy
from sensor_msgs.msg import Image,CameraInfo
from realsense2_camera_msgs.msg import Metadata,Extrinsics
from realsense2_camera_msgs.srv import DeviceInfo
from rcl_interfaces.srv import GetParameters
from rosidl_runtime_py.convert import message_to_ordereddict
from websockets.sync.client import connect

out=Path(__file__).parent
rclpy.init();node=rclpy.create_node('d405_actual_hardware_audit')
result={'streams':{},'camera_info':{},'metadata':{},'extrinsics':{},'websocket':{}}
stamps={k:[] for k in ('depth','infra1','infra2')}; arrivals={k:[] for k in stamps}
start=None;last_quality={};deadline=0
def stamp(m):return m.header.stamp.sec*10**9+m.header.stamp.nanosec
def image(k,m):
    now=time.monotonic()
    if start is None:return
    s=stamp(m);stats=result['streams'].setdefault(k,{'count':0,'nonpositive':0,'duplicates':0,'backwards':0,'gaps_over_50ms':0,'invalid_layout':0})
    stats['count']+=1
    if s<=0:stats['nonpositive']+=1
    if stamps[k]:
        delta=s-stamps[k][-1]
        stats['duplicates']+=int(delta==0);stats['backwards']+=int(delta<0);stats['gaps_over_50ms']+=int(delta>50_000_000)
    stamps[k].append(s);arrivals[k].append(now)
    stats.update(encoding=m.encoding,frame_id=m.header.frame_id,width=m.width,height=m.height,step=m.step,is_bigendian=m.is_bigendian)
    bpp=2 if m.encoding=='16UC1' else 1
    stats['invalid_layout']+=int(m.step<m.width*bpp or len(m.data)!=m.step*m.height)
    if now-last_quality.get(k,0)>1:
        last_quality[k]=now
        a=np.ndarray((m.height,m.width),dtype=('>u2' if m.is_bigendian else '<u2') if bpp==2 else 'u1',buffer=m.data,strides=(m.step,bpp))
        valid=a[a>0]
        stats.setdefault('quality_samples',[]).append({'stamp_ns':str(s),'nonzero_ratio':float(np.count_nonzero(a)/a.size),'min':int(a.min()),'max':int(a.max()),'mean':float(a.mean()),'p05_p50_p95_nonzero':np.percentile(valid,[5,50,95]).tolist() if valid.size else None})
def info(k,m):
    if k not in result['camera_info']:result['camera_info'][k]=dict(message_to_ordereddict(m))
def metadata(k,m):
    if k not in result['metadata']:result['metadata'][k]=dict(message_to_ordereddict(m))
def extrinsics(k,m):result['extrinsics'][k]=dict(message_to_ordereddict(m))
for k in stamps:
    node.create_subscription(Image,'/d405/camera/'+k+'/image_rect_raw',lambda m,k=k:image(k,m),qos_profile_sensor_data)
    node.create_subscription(CameraInfo,'/d405/camera/'+k+'/camera_info',lambda m,k=k:info(k,m),qos_profile_sensor_data)
    node.create_subscription(Metadata,'/d405/camera/'+k+'/metadata',lambda m,k=k:metadata(k,m),qos_profile_sensor_data)
for k in ('infra1','infra2'):
    node.create_subscription(Extrinsics,'/d405/camera/extrinsics/depth_to_'+k,lambda m,k=k:extrinsics(k,m),QoSProfile(depth=1,durability=DurabilityPolicy.TRANSIENT_LOCAL))
def service(cls,path,req):
    c=node.create_client(cls,path)
    if not c.wait_for_service(timeout_sec=5):raise RuntimeError('service missing '+path)
    f=c.call_async(req);rclpy.spin_until_future_complete(node,f,timeout_sec=5)
    if not f.done():raise TimeoutError(path)
    return dict(message_to_ordereddict(f.result()))
result['device_info']=service(DeviceInfo,'/d405/camera/device_info',DeviceInfo.Request())
names=['enable_depth','enable_infra1','enable_infra2','enable_color','depth_module.depth_profile','depth_module.infra_profile','depth_module.global_time_enabled','depth_module.error_polling_enabled','align_depth.enable','pointcloud__neon_.enable']
params=service(GetParameters,'/d405/camera/get_parameters',GetParameters.Request(names=names))
result['parameters']=dict(zip(names,params['values']))
warm=time.monotonic()+3
while time.monotonic()<warm:rclpy.spin_once(node,timeout_sec=.05)
result['publishers']={topic:[{'node_name':i.node_name,'node_namespace':i.node_namespace,'topic_type':i.topic_type,'qos':str(i.qos_profile)} for i in node.get_publishers_info_by_topic(topic)] for topic,_ in node.get_topic_names_and_types()}
ws_packets=[];ws_error=[]
def websocket():
    try:
        with connect('ws://127.0.0.1:9001',proxy=None,open_timeout=5) as ws:
            ws.send(json.dumps({'header':{'robot_id':'tank_inspection_robot','msg_type':'check'},'payload':{}}))
            while time.monotonic()<deadline:
                try:p=json.loads(ws.recv(timeout=.3))
                except TimeoutError:continue
                if p['header']['msg_type']=='response':result['websocket']['check']=p['payload']
                v=p['payload'].get('video')
                if v and v['camera_id']=='depth_camera':
                    a=cv2.imdecode(np.frombuffer(base64.b64decode(v['data']),np.uint8),cv2.IMREAD_UNCHANGED)
                    ws_packets.append({'at':time.monotonic(),'stamp_ns':v['stamp_ns'],'metadata':{k:x for k,x in v.items() if k!='data'},'decoded_shape':list(a.shape) if a is not None else None,'min':int(a.min()) if a is not None else None,'max':int(a.max()) if a is not None else None,'mean':float(a.mean()) if a is not None else None,'std':float(a.std()) if a is not None else None})
                    if len(ws_packets)==1:cv2.imwrite(str(out/'depth_preview.png'),a)
    except Exception as e:ws_error.append(repr(e))
start=time.monotonic();deadline=start+25;t=threading.Thread(target=websocket);t.start()
while time.monotonic()<deadline:rclpy.spin_once(node,timeout_sec=.02)
t.join(timeout=3)
for k,s in result['streams'].items():
    s['wall_interval_sec']=arrivals[k][-1]-arrivals[k][0]
    s['received_fps']=(s['count']-1)/s['wall_interval_sec']
    s['source_fps']=(s['count']-1)/((stamps[k][-1]-stamps[k][0])*1e-9)
    s['source_interval_ms_p50_p95']=np.percentile(np.diff(stamps[k])*1e-6,[50,95]).tolist()
    s['first_stamp_ns']=str(stamps[k][0]);s['last_stamp_ns']=str(stamps[k][-1])
result['matching_stamps']={k:len(set(stamps['depth'])&set(stamps[k])) for k in ('infra1','infra2')}
result['websocket'].update(count=len(ws_packets),errors=ws_error,packets=ws_packets,actual_ros_stamp_matches=sum(int(v['stamp_ns']) in stamps['depth'] for v in ws_packets),thread_alive=t.is_alive())
if len(ws_packets)>1:result['websocket']['received_fps']=(len(ws_packets)-1)/(ws_packets[-1]['at']-ws_packets[0]['at'])
result['measurement_window_sec']=25
node.destroy_node();rclpy.shutdown()
(out/'probe_result.json').write_text(json.dumps(result,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+'\n')
print(json.dumps({'streams':{k:{f:v for f,v in s.items() if f!='quality_samples'} for k,s in result['streams'].items()},'matching_stamps':result['matching_stamps'],'ws':{k:v for k,v in result['websocket'].items() if k not in ('check','packets')},'preview':ws_packets[0] if ws_packets else None},ensure_ascii=False))

            while (len(frames)<3 or eca is None) and time.monotonic()<until:

                except TimeoutError:continue

                assert isinstance(v['stamp_ns'],str) and int(v['stamp_ns'])>=STAMP_BASE and int(v['stamp_ns'])==v['stamp_sec']*10**9+v['stamp_nanosec'] and v['frame_id']=='fixture_sensor' and v['visualization_only']

            assert eca['raw']['channel_count']==8 and eca['raw']['samples_per_channel']==20

            assert report['x']==1.25 and math.isclose(report['angle'],math.pi/2) and report['angle_unit']=='rad'

    def _wait(self, timeout):
        """Internal implementation of wait() on POSIX."""
        if self.returncode is not None:
            return self.returncode
    
        if timeout is not None:
            endtime = _time() + timeout
            # Enter a busy loop if we have a timeout.  This busy loop was
            # cribbed from Lib/threading.py in Thread.wait() at r71065.
            delay = 0.0005 # 500 us -> initial delay of 1 ms

                if self._waitpid_lock.acquire(False):

                            break  # Another thread waited.

                        assert pid == self.pid or pid == 0
                        if pid == self.pid:
                            self._handle_exitstatus(sts)
                            break
